> **Summary:** 352 canonical definition records; **A / on main: 93**; **B / lane-only: 109 unique quests**; **C / no qualifying native implementation established: 150**.

> **B by branch (non-exclusive):** questpc4=2; questpc2=12; questpc3=15; questpc5=11; questpc7=15; questpc8=16; questl2=15; npcfix=3; qengine2=3; wpkg2=0; questpc=0; questpc-pc2=2; season=1; boss2=4; srvenc=0; encx=0; soulpit=0; qready1=17; qready2=18.

# Quest push audit — 2026-11-08

Main snapshot: `78294c081a099c96a57948573d5dbf0caf398abf` (after #3030). All 352 keys and display names come from its four `content/quests/definitions/quests-*.json` shards; readiness is descriptive only. Branch heads below were fetched and frozen for this audit.

A/B require a concrete native gameplay effect or quest progress/reward path, its production registration/load/caller chain, and an inspected test that invokes that path. These categories locate **implemented native slices**, not a certificate of every source/chosen narrative stage. Each positive row states its scope. Seeded prerequisite/completion state proves the named reward/action, not acquisition of that prerequisite. A takes precedence if a concrete slice is already on main; additional branch upgrades then do not inflate B counts.

This is a **static source-and-test audit**: tests were inspected, not rerun, and no CI-green claim is made. Catalogue parsing, readiness labels, keyword mentions, pure uncalled helpers, greetings alone, and tests whose production hooks are still held are not implementation evidence. For source chests, the native B3 placements were decoded at the exact claim position and checked for appearance/top-level unique selector, alongside native reward-profile deltas. Synthetic fixture selectors alone were not accepted as shipped wiring.

C is conservative: 22 rows explicitly disclose registered/partial main code without an established positive native execution proof. They must not be read as a claim that no partial code exists. The requested A/B/C taxonomy has no separate unverified bucket; these cases are visibly distinguished from rows with no native binding.
